Next lesson picks up

oneness is shown; which tawhid saves, and on what condition? (S14)

4. Tawhid that saves and carries S14 to S15

S14 کلمۂ توحید اپنے کہنے والے کا دنیا کے غم سے موت، قبر، قیامت اور حوض تک ساتھ دیتا ہے، مگر وہی توحید نجات دیتی ہے جو اہلِ بیت کی ولایت کی شرط کے ساتھ ہو، اور یہ ولایت اللہ ہی کی ولایت ہے

draft 1: H16+H17 · 11 new · 13 part-taught before · 3 taught before · 3 kept in full

Fold: the fold follows the sayer station by station (this life, death, the grave, the Day, the Pool); the condition of wilaya is met at each station and is taught in full where the Day makes it plain (steps 5 to 9). H16's question (which tawhid saves) becomes the turn of the chain, not a second lesson.

The steps 11
  1. What does the word do in this life? It relieves grief: Yunus in the whale, and Imam Sadiq's wonder at one in grief who does not say it (21:87-88); with istighfar it is the best worship (47:19).
    • U4.29-12 (JS H2 P044:13-23). new
    • U4.30-9 (EZ U4.30 P017, P018, one line; ghufayla out, Ask 28). new
    • U4.29-22. ↺ B1 S13Already taught: B1 S13 "saying «لا إله إلا الله» counts only with knowledge and belief"New here: the Kafi hadith pairing tahlil and istighfar as the best worship, with 47:19, in JS H2 P056:6-13
  2. And at death? Iblis sets a devil to push the dying to doubt; the Imams order the dying be prompted with the testimony, and with wilaya; the dying believer says the covenant prayer.
    • U4.29-13. ↺ B1 S10 · B1 S07Already taught: B1 S10 "talqin: the testimony to God, the Prophet and the Imams is prompted" (also B1 S07)New here: Iblis's devil at the moment of death and the order to prompt the dying with the shahada and with wilaya, in JS H2 P046:1-6
    • U4.29-26 (JS H3 P062:18, extended to «عبدك ورسولك», Ask 27). new
  3. In the grave? The first questions are the Lord, the Prophet and the guardian; the believer answers «الله ربي» and is made firm (14:27).
    • U4.29-15 (MM H2 P048:4). ↺ B1 S07recall only, "Recall B1 S07: after death the first questions are about the Lord, then the Prophet, then the guardian, Ali"
    • U4.29-16 (MM H2 P049:6-13). ↺ B1 S07 · B1 S10recall only, "Recall B1 S07: the two angels ask who is your Lord, your religion, your prophet; the believer answers «الله ربي»" (also B1 S10); only 14:27 is added
  4. On the Day, what is confessing tawhid worth? More than one's sins: «بل إقراركم بتوحيدي أعظم»; it is God's due not to burn the people of His tawhid; Mirza Mahdi: now the meaning of tawhid and of the muwahhid is clear, tied to the conditions in the reports.
    • U4.29-1 (MM H1 P001:3, 13-18; AH H1 P002:1-13). [NEW; full home of the Amali hadith qudsi, S08 has one line, Ask 14]
    • U4.7-5 (MM U4.7 H1 P001:19). new
    • Teacher Copy: U4.29-31. ↺ B1 S13 · B1 S02Already taught: B1 S13 "deeds have no value without prior belief in God and the Prophet" (also B1 S02)New here: the bank-account analogy, the seal of Ali's love, deeds done for a careless relative, in EZ H3 P068:1-21, P069:11-34, P070:2-15; Ask 12 recommends cut
  5. Then does a sinning muwahhid never see the Fire? He may enter but cannot stay; the unbelievers taunt him, God takes offence and brings him out; «ابدا» is about staying.
    • U4.29-2 (MM H1 P004:1, P005:8; AH H1 P002:28-30). new
  6. Who stays for ever, and what does that tell us? Ali's enemies; so the saving tawhid has a condition. God's oath: tawhid with the rope of the Ahl al-Bayt's love; "yes, when he holds to his love and wilaya"; the word is His fortress «بشروطها وأنا من شروطها»; and the root of every good is the Ahl al-Bayt, «ومن البر التوحيد».
    • U4.29-3 (MM H1 P005:4, P006:1-6; AH H1 P007:1-7; EZ H1 P010:1-28, one line at P010:24). [CORE REPEAT, kept in full: B1 S16 (and B1 S11) already teach that salvation rests on tawhid joined with the love and wilaya of the Prophet's family; new inside it: God's oath not to punish with His Fire; B1 S17 borrowed this point from Bab 4, so by rule 7 it is home here; core repeat · ask, Ask 31]
    • U4.29-4 (MM H1 P011:2-5; JS H1 P012:8-9, P013:1-10, P026:1-17). [CORE REPEAT, kept in full: same base in B1 S16, B1 S11; new inside it: "whoever says «لا إله إلا الله» enters Paradise" holds only with Ali's wilaya; core repeat · ask, Ask 31]
    • U4.1-21. ↺ B1 S11Already taught: B1 S11 "the root of religion is knowledge and wilaya; the acts are its branches"New here: Imam Sadiq's «نحن أصل كل خير ومن فروعنا كل بر ... ومن البر التوحيد», in EZ U4.1 H2 P048:1-24, trimmed (Ask 8)
    • Teacher Copy: U4.29-8. ↺ B1 S11 · B1 S13Already taught: B1 S11 "tawhid does not suffice without Ali's wilaya" (also B1 S13)New here: the Baqir telling, «إن للا إله إلا الله شروطا ألا وإني من شروطها», in JS H1 P035:2-27, P036:11-30, P038:4-20 (Ask 5)
    • Teacher Copy: U4.29-9. ↺ B1 S11 · B1 S12Already taught: B1 S11 "tawhid cut from the true Imam's wilaya fails" (also B1 S12)New here: the slogan in false custodians' hands, and Husayn separating it from them, in EZ H1 P039:4-11; Ask 12 recommends cut
    • Teacher Copy: U4.1-22. ↺ B1 S10Already taught: B1 S10 "knowing God, the Prophet and the Imam go together as one"New here: the three as one reality, submission to God's command, «الإسلام هو التسليم» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Ask 22), in EZ U4.1 H2 P049:9-11
    • Teacher Copy: U4.1-23. ↺ B1 S10Already taught: B1 S10 "loving Ali or the Prophet without God has no value"New here: the ranking question, is wilaya above tawhid, raised as its own chapter and left open, in EZ U4.1 H2 P050:1-14
  7. Is adding wilaya to tawhid not setting a partner beside God? No: only God is wali (the separating pronoun in 42:9); the Imams' wilaya is by His and along it.
    • U4.29-18 (AH H2 P051:1, P052:1-5). new
    • Teacher Copy: U4.29-17 (AH's series plan from 42:9). new
  8. How does the condition show on the Day? Those who testified to the Prophet are stopped and asked about Ali's wilaya (37:24); without it their testimony to his prophethood and to God's lordship does not benefit them.
    • U4.29-5. ↺ B2 S18 · B1 S11Already taught: B2 S18 "acknowledging the Lord does not benefit without the further covenant, prophethood and Ali's wilaya" (also B1 S11)New here: 37:24, «قفوهم إنهم مسئولون», and the stopping to ask about Ali's wilaya, in MM H1 P028:1-4 and JS H1 P031:1-14] [RULE 7, kept in full and counted as a core repeat: B1 S15 borrowed this point from Bab 4; core repeat · ask, Ask 34
    • Teacher Copy: U4.29-23. ↺ B2 S13 · B3 S18Already taught: B2 S13 "the Resurrection crier's «الله أكبر» that the deniers cannot say" (also B3 S18)New here: the further calls, «لا إله إلا الله» separating the polytheists, and the stages after, in JS H3 P057:1-38 (the same Tafsir al-'Askari report as 37:24, Ask 12)
  9. Is «علي ولي الله» then a third word beside the two? It is written with them where God wrote: on the Throne, and on the Prophet's ring, «كتبت ما شئت وكتبنا ما شئنا».
    • AH H1 P030:2-5 (inside the U4.29-4 block, Jami's Throne wording, Ask 21).
    • U4.29-28. ↺ B2 S03 · B1 S11Already taught: B2 S03 "the testimony is complete only with «علي ولي الله»" (also B1 S11)New here: the story of the Prophet's ring in Jami's telling, in EZ H3 P065:1-18 (Ask 11)
    • Held, Teacher Copy flagged: U4.29-21. ↺ B1 S12 · B1 S09Already taught: B1 S12 "diverting the right from Ali wronged tawhid itself" (also B1 S09)New here: the damage reaches every part of Islam, and wilaya and bara'a are paired, in JS H2 P055:1-27
    • Held, Teacher Copy flagged: U4.29-29. ↺ B2 S17 · B1 S11Already taught: B2 S17 "God's oneness, Muhammad's prophethood and Ali's wilaya are taken together" (also B1 S11)New here: Uqba ibn Amir's pledge and the wasiyya as a pillar like oneness, in EZ H3 P066:7-10
    • Held, Teacher Copy flagged: U4.29-30 (the objector of 70:1-3). new
  10. So what is the condition at heart? Sincerity: said sincerely the word earns Paradise and erases sins, said falsely it protects only blood and property; with two limits, the one who could not understand is excused, and people's rights remain.
    • U4.29-11. ↺ B1 S13Already taught: B1 S13 "the testimony is real only from the heart; outward submission differs from faith"New here: Saduq's bab 1 on the reward of the muwahhidun, Paradise and sins erased if sincere, blood and property only if false, the hundred times, in JS H1 P041:1-37, P042:2-13, P043:2-8, P015:4-15
    • Teacher Copy: U4.29-20 (AH H2 P054:1-6). ↺ B1 S10recall only, "Recall B1 S10: all faith is faith in the unseen, God, the Prophet's prophethood and the Imam's wilaya"
  11. Close: what opens the gates of Paradise, and who is welcomed at the Pool? A testimony said truly; the people of the word of tawhid who held the Book and loved the Prophet's progeny.

Next lesson picks up

where is the word said every day, and which formulas, prayers and rites carry it? (S15)

S15 اذان، صلوات، نماز، حج اور دعا کے کلمات ہر روز توحید کا اعلان کرتے ہیں اور بندہ خدا کے حضور توحید ہی لے کر جاتا ہے

draft 1: H18 · 18 new · 3 part-taught before · 2 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Why does the faith put tawhid into words said aloud every day? Slogans carry belief and are honoured (22:32) (EZ U4.30-1 P008:4, P008:8, P019:23-25; U4.30-2 P021:3-4; U4.30-5). new
  2. What does the adhan declare first? «الله اکبر» and «لا إله إلا الله», raised against the voice of the idols; the adhan is part of tawhid, not the other way round; Bilal's adhan made the outward converts cover their ears; the Prophet's adhan on the Mi'raj repeated by the angels; no one needs anyone but God, the One, the Subduer (EZ U4.30-1 P008:9-13, P019:9-12, U4.30-2 P020:2-3; U4.30-5, -10, -11; EZ U4.6-42 U4.6-3 P061; MM U4.7-21 U4.7-2 P035; MM U4.3-11 U4.3-1 P028:1-3). new AH's takbir line (U4.6-2) is homed in S06: one line pointing there.
  3. Why does the salawat stand beside the adhan? [PART: recall B1 S12 "the salawat gathers tawhid (Allahumma), prophethood, imamate and the return, and raising it is the Shia slogan" then new: the request itself presupposes a God who is not unjust, so all five principles stand in one sentence, while the takbir carries tawhid alone (EZ U4.30-1 P001:7-9; P010:1-9; U4.30-6)]. U4.1-10 and U4.30-1 are SAME: recall only.
  4. Can a worshipper truly say "You alone we worship"? Said alone it would be a lie; he says it in company with Muhammad and his family, and the final salam thanks them (EZ U4.30-1 P007:32-45, 49-52; U4.30-4). new
  5. Which words of prayer draw the border of tawhid and shirk? Ikhlas states it and Kafirun separates it, so once begun they are not switched; where God says "say I", say «أشهد أن لا إله إلا الله» without egoism, where He says "say He", say «قل هو الله أحد»; the four tasbihat declare Him pure (EZ U4.30-1 P011:6, P012:2-10, P014:8-9; U4.30-7; EZ U4.7-41 U4.7-4 P068; EZ U4.6-33 U4.6-3 P047). new (Four tasbihat: one home in S06, Ask 14.)
  6. What does the pilgrim answer? ↺ B2 S03Already taught: B2 S03 "labbayk is said to God, not to a land, a nation or an assembly"New here: «لا شريك لك» is the heart of ihram; the pilgrim answers not Abraham the herald but the God of Abraham, after laying aside the self he saw in his clothes; at the Ka'ba the same pilgrim says «لا إله إلا الله وحده» (EZ U4.30-1 P002:2-11, P002:36-56, P005:1-11; JS U4.6-1 P010:2-30; AH U4.5-1 P014:1-6; U4.30-2, U4.6-8, U4.5-6)

Who first affirmed this word beside the Prophet, and did he ever turn from it? (S16)

5. Witnesses and the fruit S16 to S20

S16 بیتِ رسول صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ توحید کا پہلا گواہ ہے: علی علیہ السلام نے رسولِ خدا صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ کے ساتھ سب سے پہلے خدا کو ایک مانا اور پلک جھپکنے کو بھی شرک نہ کیا

draft 1: H19 · 6 new · 6 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. Who first affirmed oneness beside the Prophet? Ali: the Prophet's own clause «وأول من وحد الله معي» (Jami, Ask 20); he said la ilaha illa Allah and prayed seven years before others, when God was wholly worshipped only in the house of the Prophet, Khadija and Ali (EZ U4.31-1 P008:14-16, P009:5-6; EZ U4.31-3 P061, P063). [PART: recall B1 S13 "Ali had no past of idol worship or kufr" then new: the title "first who affirmed God's oneness with His Messenger" (P008:14-16; Jordac's first-prostration contrast P008:15-16 in the Teacher Copy) (U4.31-2)]; U4.31-27 new.
  2. Was this first faith marked before and accepted? The monk sent Abu Talib his testimony of oneness (one line, s42), and in al-Ma'mun's debate Ishaq b. Hammad conceded that sincerity in the testimony was the best deed when the Prophet was sent (EZ U4.31-3 P064 s42, P065 one line; U4.31-28, -29, Teacher Copy). new
  3. Did the Prophet's house testify from the first breath? Ali at birth gave adhan and iqama «بالحنفية» and testified to oneness; Fatima at birth said «أشهد أن لا إله إلا الله» (EZ U4.31-1 P018:4-6, P020:5-13; JS P016:6-9, P017:6-13; U4.31-8). new
  4. Did Ali ever lapse? Never for the blink of an eye, so at the end he said «فزت ورب الكعبة»: a test passed in full (EZ U4.31-1 P010:3-12, 20-21; U4.31-3). [Core repeat kept in full, flagged: B1 S14 and B1 S17 already teach it] With AH's line: ↺ B1 S17Already taught: B1 S17 "Ali never associated anything with God for the blink of an eye"New here: the elders' secret du'a for the reappearance asks by the spirit of Ali, a monotheist and worshipper of the Samad from the womb to his death (AH U4.32-1 P008:8-11; U4.32-3).
  5. Did he say it of himself? One couplet: I affirmed him when all were astray, praise to God who has no partner; the Prophet said "you spoke truly" (EZ U4.31-3 P076, cited «منسوب»; U4.31-38). new
  6. How did they teach it? Every address opened with tawhid. Ghadir: [PART: recall B1 S12 "the Ghadir opening «علا في توحده ودنا في تفرده» (used there for the value of memorising)" then new: what it says of God, «لم يكن له ضد ولا معه ند», One, Samad, who neither begets nor was begotten (EZ U4.31-1 P013:10-21; U4.31-5)]. Jamal: ↺ taught beforerecall only, U4.31-4. Fatima's sermon: [PART, Teacher Copy: recall B1 S12 "Fatima's Fadak sermon carries a tawhid beyond the philosophers" then new: the challenge to bring from Aristotle, Plato, Ibn Sina or Farabi a word on oneness as beautiful as hers (EZ U4.31-1 P040:2-15; U4.31-12)]. Teacher Copy: the Ghadir-Friday sermon, praise made without need of praisers, as a way of confessing His divinity and samadiyya (JS U4.7-3 P062:1; U4.7-36) new.
  7. Even in war and in succession? Siffin: ↺ B1 S12 · B3 S07Already taught: B1 S12 and B3 S07 "Ali's words when setting out for battle are full of tawhid"New here: this sermon's own order, praise for blessings, reliance, «ثم إني أشهد أن لا إله إلا الله وحده لا شريك له», and the call to obey your Lord against the enemy's call (EZ U4.31-1 P042:4-8, P045:12-13; U4.31-13)]. Imam Hasan: [PART: recall B1 S12 "Imam Hasan's words on tawhid are lofty and match Ali's" then new: his sermon after Ali's martyrdom, «الحمد لله الذي كان في أوليته وحدانيا في أزليته», creating with no prior model (EZ U4.31-2 P046:2-8; U4.31-14).

Texts and Jami results 8
  1. «وأول من وحد الله معي» (the Prophet; Hilyat al-Abrar 2:36-37, 2:440; Tafsir al-Hibri 1:402, 582) in place of the third-person «اول من وحد الله مع رسوله» (H1 P008); the ten-title list is in no Jami source (Ask 20).
  2. «إن محمدا لما دعا إلى الإيمان بالله والتوحيد له كنا أهل البيت» (Nahj letter; Bihar 33:111-112; Waq'at Siffin 89-90): FOUND; «فلبثنا», not «فلبسنا» (Ask 21).
  3. «وهو يؤذن ويقيم بالحنفية ويشهد بوحدانية الله» (Rawdat al-Wa'izin 204): FOUND. Ali's birth shahada (Nukhab al-Manaqib 1:591). «فنطقت فاطمة ... أشهد أن لا إله إلا الله» (Ibn Shahrashub, al-Manaqib 79): FOUND.
  4. «لم يشرك بالله طرفة عين أبدا» (H1 P010:6-10): FOUND-OTHER-SPEAKER, authors' words only (Ghayat al-Maram 5:166 and others), spelled «طرفة» (Ask 22). «فزت ورب الكعبة»: FOUND (al-Manaqib 337-338, 493). U4.32-3's du'a «اللهم إني أسألك بروح علي بن أبي طالب»: NOT-FOUND as a du'a; the lesson itself calls it the elders' du'a.
  5. «الحمد لله الذي علا في توحده ودنا في تفرده» (Ghadir; Iqbal al-a'mal 2): FOUND. Printed again flagged (B1 S12). S17 points back.
  6. «دعوه فإن الذي يريده الأعرابي هو الذي نريده من القوم» (Jamal): FOUND (al-Shafi fi Sharh al-Kafi 2:8 and others). Now under the SAME recall only.
  7. «ثم إني أشهد أن لا إله إلا الله وحده لا شريك له» (Siffin; Bihar 32:616-617, Ghayat al-Maram 2:77): FOUND, Jami excerpt. «الحمد لله الذي كان في أوليته وحدانيا في أزليته» (Imam Hasan; Bihar 43:363-364): FOUND.
  8. Other texts, as draft 1: the newborn reciting the scriptures (Rawdat al-Wa'izin 204-205; «شيث», Ask 11); the end of the Siffin sermon «وقد حضركم عدوكم» (Ask 10); Ghadir «لم يكن له ضد ولا معه ند» (Rawdat al-Wa'izin 219-220, Ask 8); the monk's blocks (Bihar 35:11-12, 35:101-102; «المثرم», Ask 12); the three siddiqs (al-Khisal 1:184); the couplet P076 (Jami has it, Ask 14); «فلا مبدل لخلقه» (Bihar 43:363-364, Ask 15); U4.7-36 «من غير حاجة منه إلى حامديه» (Iqbal 2, Bihar 94:112-113), printed again flagged (B1 S16); «اخرج ... أن يراك قتيلا» (Bihar 44:364, Ask 23).
Next lesson picks up

The house testified first; who else kept the word alive against tyranny and torture, and at what price? (S17)

S17 ائمہ علیہم السلام نے حکومت کے لیے نہیں بلکہ لا الٰہ الا اللہ کے لیے قیام کیا، اور ان کے ساتھ اہلِ ایمان نے بھی ظلم، اذیت اور موت کی گھڑی تک اسے تھامے رکھا؛ اسی قربانی سے یہ کلمہ دنیا میں زندہ ہے

draft 1: H20+H21 · 12 new · 9 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 1 kept in full

Fold: three movements: the Imams rose for the word and paid for it (steps 1 to 5); believers of every kind held it under the same pressure, to the last breath (steps 6 to 9); and the verdict: the word lives (step 10).

The steps 10
  1. What did the Imams rise for? Not rule: rule is worth less than a worn shoe unless it establishes «لا إله إلا الله» and acquaints people with God; Husayn's message to the pilgrims says the aim was God's religion (EZ U4.31-2 P060:5-17; EZ U4.31-1 P014:4-17, 19; one telling of both). [PART: recall B1 S12 "Ali fought for beliefs, not rule; the aim was God's religion and tawhid" then new: the worn-shoe saying (P060:9-17, setting per Jami, Ask 24) (U4.31-26) and Husayn's own message (P014:4-17) (U4.31-6)]. Import U13.29/1 kept, flagged (below).
  2. Why could Husayn not keep taqiyya? Yazid would force people back to idols, and his blood was not dearer to him than his Lord, His tawhid and his grandfather's message (MM U4.7-1 P012:1-7; U4.7-6). new Rule 7: B1 S14 borrowed this point from Bab 4; Bab 4 is its home and teaches it here in full.
  3. What were his last words? In the pit: content with Your decree, «لا معبود سواك» (JS U4.31-1 P021:4-14, P024:8-12; EZ P027:7-12, P031:4-17; AH RR5-009), flagged "from the maqatil, no narrated source in Jami" (Ask 27). [PART: recall B1 S14 "the infallibles' words at death: content with Your decree" then new: the full munajat and its «لا معبود سواك» (U4.31-9)]. Import U4.28-4 kept beside it, flagged (below).
  4. Did the house of Husayn carry it into captivity and home again? At Yazid's court Imam Sajjad, fresh from the iron collar, answered the muezzin's testimony and showed that the true monotheists are the Ahl al-Bayt; back in Medina he rebuilt religion beginning with tawhid (EZ U4.31-2 P047:5-12, P058:1-9; EZ U4.1-2 P047:1-7). ↺ B1 S12Already taught: B1 S12 "Imam Sajjad taught knowing God and tawhid in Damascus before Yazid, and put that knowledge first"New here: his completing of the muezzin's testimony (P047:5-12, told with Jami's scene, Ask 17) (U4.31-15); fresh from the collar, the Ahl al-Bayt as the true monotheists (P058:1-9) (U4.31-24); the Medina programme in order, tawhid, prophethood, return, ethics, rights (U4.1-2 P047:1-7) (U4.1-20)
  5. Was their worship for God alone, in ease as in trial? Ali worshipped God because He is worthy; Imam Sajjad: You are my paradise; Kumayl asks whether the Fire will touch tongues that spoke His oneness truly; Husayn at 'Arafa: no god other than He, nothing equals Him (AH RR10-012, RR10-014; EZ U4.30-2 P028 to P030 (U4.30-17), P027 (U4.30-16)). new Teacher Copy: Ali's verse «عند العلي الواحد الموحد» («منسوب», U4.30-20) new.
  6. Could ordinary believers hold it under a tyrant? Asiya told Pharaoh he was no god; under torture she kept saying la ilaha illa Allah and asked for a house near God (66:11); against her, the one who worships God «على حرف» (22:11). In Mecca Bilal under the stone: «أحد أحد»; Yasir and Sumayya killed: «صبرا يا آل ياسر» (JS H1 P001:8-15, P003:8-27, P015:1-13; EZ U4.31-1 P005 to P007; U4.31-1). new
  7. Is outward denial under compulsion a betrayal, and what was their crime? Ammar's denial was lawful (16:106) and Bilal's refusal too: two lawful choices. Their only crime was faith: the People of the Ditch fought no one (85:4-8) (EZ U4.31-1 P006:1-12, U4.31-2 P049:24-31; JS U4.31-2 P050, P051; U4.31-17). U4.31-17 new; ↺ B3 S19Already taught: B3 S19 "the People of the Ditch (85:4-8), and God's respite to tyrants"New here: they fought no one and were burned only for saying God is One (JS U4.31-2 P050:1-12, P051:5-57) (U4.31-18).
  8. Is the word kept in plenty too, and to the last breath? The Bedouin enriched through Fatima's necklace: "You are a God we did not newly invent" (EZ U4.30-2 P031; U4.30-18); the captive spared for his good traits asks how to become Muslim and says the shahada (JS U4.31-2 P057; U4.31-23). Teacher Copy: Shaykh Abbas Qummi's dhikr lighting a dark lane (U4.31-21), the dying Sharif told to testify to each Imam (U4.31-22). new Imports U11.25/44 and U13.36/17 kept, flagged (below).
  9. Are the faithful alone in saying it? Creation says it too: the tree came at Imam Kazim's call (in Jami it comes and goes and says nothing, Ask 41). [PART: recall B1 S10 "the lizard and the pebble testified to God's oneness and the mission" then new: the tree before Imam Kazim and its Quranic parallel (JS U4.31-2 P052:1-15) (U4.31-19); Abu Qubays at Abu Jahl's demand (JS U4.31-2 P053), Teacher Copy only, not found in Jami (U4.31-20)]. Imports U4.27-15 and U4.27-20 kept, flagged (below). Ask 3: this step may go to the Teacher Copy.
  10. Did it all succeed? «حتى أعلنتم دعوته»: through the Imams' sacrifice the world knows Islam calls to tawhid; the Abbasids could not erase tawhid, qibla or Quran, «والله متم نوره» (EZ U4.31-1 P033 to P039; U4.31-10, -11). new Teacher Copy: Mecca guards tawhid as the shrines guard wilaya (U4.31-25) new.

Next lesson picks up

The Imams and the faithful kept the word; did the Prophet himself teach it as the first article, and did the Imams keep it at the head of all they said? (S18)

S18 توحید کی گواہی رسولِ خدا صلی اللہ علیہ و آلہ نے سب سے پہلا عقیدہ بنا کر سکھائی، ائمہ علیہم السلام نے اسے ہر کلام کے آغاز اور آخری سانس تک نبھایا، اور اہلِ ایمان نے اس کی قیمت دی

draft 1: H22 · 15 new · 7 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Did the Prophet ask for this testimony and teach it as the first article? He took it from the people, hand on his breast, «وأنا أشهد معكم» (EZ U4.31-4 P083:8-13; U4.31-43; setting per Jami, Ask 18) new; on the eve of Uhud he taught Hamza oneness first, then risala, ma'ad, wilaya ↺ B1 S10Already taught: B1 S10 "on the eve of Uhud the Prophet asked Hamza what he would answer his Lord"New here: the order taught, beginning with God's oneness (EZ U4.31-4 P087, P090, P092 to P094; U4.31-45)]; the talqin keeps the same order as a formula [SAME: recall only, U4.31-16.
  2. Did the Imams keep it at the head of what they said? Ali's first sermon as ruler «إلها واحدا صمدا» ↺ B1 S12Already taught: B1 S12 "the first caliphs left nothing on knowing God beside Ali's words"New here: the sermon's text set against their sermons (EZ U4.31-3 P066:4-13; U4.31-30)]; the letter to Mu'awiya opening «الذي لا إله إلا هو» (EZ P068 s2, P069:2-3; U4.31-32) new; after the Arbitration «لا شريك له», no complaint against God (EZ P071:3-12; U4.31-34) new; one line each for the battle du'a [PART: recall B1 S12 and B3 S07 "Ali's words going to battle are full of tawhid" then new: «اللهم يا أحد يا صمد» against the oppressors (EZ P073:11-14; U4.31-36)] and sermon 91 (JS U4.7-3 P049 to P053; U4.7-29) new. Teacher Copy: U4.7-35, U4.7-37, U4.31-35, U4.31-39 new; the dotless sermon [PART, Teacher Copy: recall B1 S12 "the dotless sermon as a display of his tawhid knowledge" then new: its content (EZ P074:1-7, P075:2-3; U4.31-37).
  3. And at the end of life? Ali's will opens with the testimony and he died saying «لا إله إلا الله»; Imam Hasan's will: «لا شريك له في الملك ولا ولي له من الذل» (EZ U4.31-4 P078:9-11, 44, P079:8-10, P081:11-13, P082:6-8; U4.31-40, -42). new
  4. Why say aloud what no believer doubted? Propaganda had cast doubt on their Islam, so Fatima in the mosque and Husayn in his testament spoke first as witnesses of oneness ↺ B1 S15Already taught: B1 S15 "Fatima, like Husayn, set out knowing God against the propaganda of the time"New here: they opened with the testimony itself, «أن الحسين يشهد أن لا إله إلا الله» (EZ U4.31-4 P084:5-15, P086:1-7; U4.31-44).
  5. If the other side says the very same words, what is the difference? Mu'awiya opened his letter to Qays with the same formula, and both armies gave the same adhan; Imam Hasan's answer: people agree on the testimony and split over the sunan, wilaya among them (EZ U4.31-3 P070:8-16; U4.31-4 P100, P080:1-9; U4.31-33, -49, -41). new
  6. Is knowing these beliefs only a step to practice? No, it is religion itself ↺ B1 S01Already taught: B1 S01 "beliefs are valuable in themselves, not a preliminary to rulings"New here: in Ziyarat Al Yasin we make the Imam witness to «لا إله إلا هو وحده لا شريك له» and «لا حبيب إلا هو وأهله», and even Moses and Jesus must hold these beliefs (EZ U4.31-4 P096:3-12; P097:1-13 Teacher Copy; U4.31-46).
  7. What did the faithful pay for it? Salman [PART: recall B1 S10 "Salman was tortured by his parents for leaving Zoroastrianism for monotheism" then new: he was no soldier passing Iranian secrets but a poor monotheist (EZ U4.31-4 P098:9-18; U4.31-47), Ask 3]; Qays ibn Mushir on the Kufa pulpit: flesh and bone bear witness (EZ P099:6-11; U4.31-48) new.
  8. Close: and if all turn away? Say «لا إله إلا الله» alone, as Abraham did and became a nation (EZ U4.31-4 P101:9-13; U4.31-50). new

Next lesson picks up

Once the word is taught, kept and paid for, what does it ask first of the one who says it, and what does living on it make of him? (S19)

S19 کلمۂ توحید کا پہلا حکم یہ ہے کہ بندگی صرف اسی ایک کی ہو، اور اس پر جینا انسان کے ہر عمل، اس کے معاشرے اور اس کی میراث کو اسی کلمے کا پابند بنا دیتا ہے

draft 1: H23+H24 · 14 new · 5 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 1 kept in full

Fold: two movements: what the word asks (steps 1 to 5: worship Him alone, because He alone is worthy, said truly), then what living on it makes of a person, a people and their heirs (steps 6 to 11).

The steps 11
  1. What is the first thing God decreed? That we worship none but Him, then kindness to parents (17:23, cut to two clauses) [PART: recall B1 S02 "kindness to parents ranks right after knowing God" then new: 17:23 as the first decree, no shirk and worship of Him in His oneness, before parents (AH U4.32-1 P001, P002; JS P003 to P005; U4.32-1)]; 2:83 says it as a report, not a prohibition, to stress the command (JS U4.32-2 P033:1-30; U4.32-19) new. AH RR10-010: we came only to serve God.
  2. Why is no one but He worthy of that worship? [PART: recall B3 S14 "a prisoner cannot free a prisoner (Rumi)" then new: a mortal like Pharaoh, himself a prisoner of death, cannot free another, so only the One who neither begets nor is begotten is worthy (AH U4.32-2 P027:1-5; U4.32-13)]; the Prophet taught «إياك نعبد» as «نعبد واحدا», not as the Dahriyya, the dualists, the polytheists, Jews or Christians (JS U4.32-2 P032:1-5; U4.32-18) new.
  3. Who gave you all you have? At each step from the womb to sight and speech the answer is God alone, so worship and shame before Him follow (AH re-read RR2-001, RR5-006, RR2-002, RR7-010). new
  4. Why will some not say it? The self's «میں»: the arrogant man answers "Me? I am not ready", with two statues of himself (AH U4.32-1 P021:1-4; U4.32-11). new
  5. What does saying it truly look like? Bilal said it with all his limbs; «إياك نعبد» said morning, noon and night forbids backbiting, slander, ill temper, unlawful music (AH U4.32-2 P028; JS U4.32-1 P009:4-41; U4.32-14, -4). new
  6. What can living on it cost? Asiya, nailed by hands and feet, would not turn back: «إن الذين قالوا ربنا الله ثم استقاموا» (41:30) (AH U4.32-1 P007, shortened; U4.32-2) new; her story is told in S17, one line back. Rule 7: B1 S14 borrowed this point from Bab 4 (framed there as yaqin); here, its home, it is taught in full in its own sense, steadfastness. Husayn bore all to guard tawhid [PART: recall B3 S18 "Husayn's stand was over God and revelation against Yazid" then new: he bore all to guard tawhid and the mission from a plot that had reached cursing Ali, so the event must be kept alive, with the ziyarat «وبذل مهجته فيك» (JS U4.32-1 P010:2-6, P011:1-8, P012:1-15; U4.32-5)]; MM's Husayn point (U4.7-6) is in S17, one line back.
  7. What does living on it look like each day? Hold God present and be ashamed before Him; with God in view lying, usury and the forbidden are left with ease; «يا غلام خف الله تجده أمامك ... يكفك ما سواه» (AH re-read RR3-011, RR3-012, RR3-013, RR3-014, RR6-009, RR6-018). new
  8. Does it bind us to people too? God bound the rights of Muslims with sincerity and tawhid, so Ali let no Muslim's honour be violated (EZ U4.31-3 P067:5-17; U4.31-31) new; Islam in sum ↺ B1 S01Already taught: B1 S01 "the two axes: knowing the Lord and serving His creatures"New here: worship of the One with service of people grounded in it, «فصل لربك وانحر» (108:2), hence prayer and zakat paired through the Quran (JS U4.32-1 P022 to P025; U4.32-12).
  9. What does the word make of a people? At Hajj all say «لا إله إلا الله وحده» and the lustful glance is gone; one tongue of tawhid from Africa to Paris (AH U4.32-2 P029, P030 merged; U4.32-15, -16). new
  10. What do we hand on? Marry to bring sayers of the word (Salman would not curse his torturers); write your tawhid into your will as the righteous father under the wall and Imam Hasan did (EZ U4.32-2 P035 to P039; U4.32-21, -22). new
  11. How do we leave, and greet those who left? The Pen was first told to write this word; we ask to die saying it; at the graves we greet «أهل لا إله إلا الله» (AH U4.32-2 P031; EZ U4.32-1 P018 to P020; U4.32-17, -10). new Teacher Copy: U4.1-11 taught before; U4.1-15 [PART: recall B1 S08 "sound belief is among the greatest blessings" then new: what thanking it requires (JS U4.1-2 P042:1-16)]; U4.32-20 (closing du'a) and U4.32-23 (bay'a) new.

If the word is lived and handed on, will it one day be the word of the whole earth, and by whose hand? (S20)

S20 خدا کا وعدہ ہے کہ امام مہدی علیہ السلام کے ہاتھوں زمین کا کوئی گوشہ ایسا نہ رہے گا جہاں کلمۂ توحید نہ پکارا جائے

draft 1: H25 · 2 new · 8 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. The word has the whole Bab behind it: will it ever be the word of all the earth? Yes, by God's promise (24:55; 9:33): in the hadith God swears to raise His word through the Imams, and "My word" is tawhid (AH U4.33 P001:47-48, P003:2; EZ P017:9-11; JS U4.32-1 P013:1-6; U4.32-6) new. One recall line opens the lesson: "Recall B2 S12: under the Mahdi the world turns to tawhid" (the base every PART point below shares).
  2. What exactly is promised? No land left where the shahada is not called; idol, star and fire worship end ↺ Bab 1 to 3Already taught: base as step 1,New here: Islam made to prevail over all religions (9:33) and the worship of idols, stars and fire swept away (AH U4.33 P003:2, P004:1-2; U4.33-1)]; the «لا يبقى أرض» block (EZ P010:10, P013:3; Jami's speaker Imam al-Sadiq, Ask 3); Abu Basir cut (EZ P014:4-5); Teacher Copy: the long occultation before it [PART: base as step 1, then new: it comes after a long occultation that tests who obeys God in the unseen (JS U4.32-1 P017:10; U4.32-9).
  3. Who does it, and is it only a Shia belief? The Mahdi of the Prophet's line, reported in the Sunni sahih books too ↺ Bab 1 to 3Already taught: base as step 1,New here: the Sunni sahih reports and «يملأ الأرض قسطا وعدلا» (AH U4.33 P001:1, P002:1-3; RR10-008, RR4-002; U4.33-1)]; through him God gathers creation on His tawhid [PART: base as step 1, then new: the Mi'raj hadith's «حتى يعلن دعوتي ويجمع الخلق على توحيدي» (JS U4.32-1 P014:1-14, P015:1-15; U4.32-7)] and makes the earth flourish with His tasbih, taqdis, tahlil, takbir and tamjid, His word highest [PART: base as step 1, then new: the earth purified of His enemies (JS U4.32-1 P016:1-11; U4.32-8).
  4. Has it ever happened? No: never one rule over all the earth; the promise still waits (EZ U4.33 P010:12-13, P011:5-6, P017:10-11, once). Teacher Copy: ↺ Bab 1 to 3Already taught: base as step 1,New here: the future belongs not to Christians, communists or materialists but to the Mahdi, and life will not end before the call of tawhid fills the earth (EZ U4.33 P016:3-19, P017:1-11; U4.33-2).
  5. Why is it a burden no prophet or Imam carried? [PART: base as step 1, then new: the Prophet made some millions Muslim in 23 years; the Imam must bring five continents to tawhid and justice (AH U4.33 P018:1-8, P019:1-7, P020:5, P020:17-24, P021:1-5; U4.33-3)]; Nudba line once.
  6. How will it be done? ↺ Bab 1 to 3Already taught: base as step 1,New here: by sound reasoning that makes the divine ma'arif clear, so Christians, Jews, Zoroastrians and their clergy say la ilaha illa Allah; force only for those who cannot understand reason (AH U4.33 P023:1-10; U4.33-5).
  7. What does his title say? [PART: base as step 1, then new: he gathers people on monotheism, «جامع الكلمة على التقوى» (Jami: Du'a al-Nudba, not the Quran, Ask 8), and in his time both hemispheres say la ilaha illa Allah (AH U4.33 P022:1-2; U4.33-4)]; close: tawhid gives man worth, no prostration to wood, stone or fire (EZ P012:4-9, one line).

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. 9:33 «هُوَ الَّذِي أَرْسَلَ رَسُولَهُ ... لِيُظْهِرَهُ عَلَى الدِّينِ كُلِّهِ» (JS P013:1); also used by U4.5-2 (S05): if S05 prints it in full, one line pointing back. Ayah; Tanzil check pending.
  2. 24:55 «وَعَدَ اللَّهُ الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا مِنكُمْ ... يَعْبُدُونَنِي لَا يُشْرِكُونَ بِي شَيْئًا» (EZ P017:9-11), as a block. Ayah; Tanzil check pending.
  3. «لا يبقى أرض إلا نودي فيها بشهادة أن لا إله إلا الله وأن محمدا رسول الله»: FOUND-OTHER-SPEAKER; Imam al-Sadiq on 3:83 ('Ayyashi via Nur al-Thaqalayn 1:362) and Imam al-Baqir (Bihar 53:113); «أرض», not «قرية» (Ask 3).
  4. Abu Basir «ولا تبقى في الأرض بقعة عبد فيها غير الله ... ويكون الدين كله لله» (Kamal al-din via Bihar 51:146): FOUND, cut.
  5. «يملأ الأرض قسطا وعدلا كما ملئت ظلما وجورا» (AH P002:1) and the Sunni form «لو لم يبق من الدهر إلا يوم ...» (al-Durr al-nazim 1:754; al-Fusul al-muhimma 1109 citing Abu Dawud): FOUND.
  6. Mi'raj hadith «... ولأعلين بهم كلمتي ... حتى يعلن دعوتي ويجمع الخلق على توحيدي ... وبالقائم منكم أعمر أرضي بتسبيحي ... وكلمتي العليا» (JS P013 to P016): FOUND in parts (Amali of Saduq 449-450; Nur al-thaqalayn 3:125-126); the Qa'im block FOUND-OTHER-WORDING (Ask 19). Quote once.
  7. «فلا يبقى في الأرض معبود دون الله ... وذلك بعد غيبة طويلة» (JS P017:10): FOUND (I'lam al-wara 2:287-293; al-Ghayba of Tusi 2:427).
Next lesson picks up

S20 closes Bab 4 and the hub. It leaves the learner with the question S19 answered for now: how to live by the word while the promise still waits.

How draft 2 was made

What draft 2 does (the rules applied)
  1. SAME point (already taught in Bab 1 to 3): not taught again. One recall line naming the earlier lesson; the scholar's repeat sits under it in the Teacher Copy as a flagged ask, so nothing is cut on Claude's word.
  2. PART point: a one-line recall of the shared base, then the new part taught in full in the scholar's own new wording (each entry names the paragraphs that carry it).
  3. Core repeat: a repeat that is the lesson's own claim stays in full, flagged core repeat · ask; Ali decides which Bab carries it in full.
  4. Arabic printed in full in Bab 1 to 3: printed again in this Bab's Teacher Copy, flagged with the earlier lesson.
  5. Imports from Bab 1 and 2 dropped (7): U1.8.2, UN2.12.19, UN2.3.4 (draft-1 H13, now S11); U1.1.13, U1.1.17, U1.1.52, U1.11.22 (draft-1 H22, now S18).
  6. Imports taken only to bring in AH or MM (the other 23 rows): kept only where a sound framing supports the step, each with a line Claude's judgement; otherwise dropped, with the reason.
  7. Points Bab 1 to 3 borrowed from Bab 4 (OVERLAP section 1) stay taught here in full (Bab 4 is their home), flagged with an ASK even where judged SAME or PART.
